How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Fairy Griffin Skin
Categories: Mythic items, Pet Skins, Pages with UIs
[Fairy Griffin Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_SKIN_GRIFFIN_FAIRY
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Griffin pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 20 fairy themed griffins!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Fairy Griffin Skin is an [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Fairy Griffin Skin could be purchased from the Hypixel Store during a 2026 Hypixel Store Halloween promotion.
## Usage
The Fairy Griffin Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Griffin Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 20 fairy themed griffins!

# Fake Neuroscience Degree
Categories: Mythic items, Items
[Fake Neuroscience Degree]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FAKE_NEUROSCIENCE_DEGREE
Tooltip: Use at (the real) Kat to upgrade a Spider or Tarantula pet to Mythic rarity.; With this fake diploma you can somehow talk spiders into uh...; upgrading their rarity.; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X MYTHIC
The Fake Neuroscience Degree is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
A Fake Neuroscience Degree can be purchased from [[Kat (Rift)|Neuroscientist Kat]] for 10 [[Volt Crux|Volt Cruxes]], 10 [[Shadow Crux|Shadow Cruxes]] and 5,000 [[Motes]] after having deposited 30 Flies, 40 Spiders and 60 Silverfish in the [[Kat (Rift)|Vermin Bin]] with a [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]].
## Usage
The Fake Neuroscience Degree can be given to [[Kat]] as well as 1,000,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade both [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Spider Pet]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Tarantula Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Spider Pet]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Tarantula Pet]] both unlock the following perk:
|Perk|[Lv1-100]|
|-|-|
|Web Battlefield|Killing mobs grant +6 [[Strength]] and +1 [[Magic Find]] for 40s to all players staying within 20 blocks of where they died. Stacks up to 10 times.|
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Fake Neuroscience Degree can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Fake Neuroscience Degree can be given to [[Kat]] to upgrade a [[Spider Pet|Spider]] or [[Tarantula Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rarity]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Fake Neuroscience Degree.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Increased the rarity of Fake Neuroscience Degree from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Fake Neuroscience Degree can now be seen in the items [[SkyBlock API]].|
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Fake Neuroscience Degree.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Increased the rarity of Fake Neuroscience Degree from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
Linked from: [[Kat]], [[Kat (Rift)]], [[Kat/Costs]], [[Pet Upgrades]], [[Pets]], [[Rat Jetpack]], [[Rift Dimension]], [[Spider Pet]], [[Tarantula Pet]]

Forest Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Forest Dye can be purchased from the Spring 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Forest Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #1D3451 and ⁠ #1E3953. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 181,760x (2,840 stacks (181,760 items total)) [[Lily Pad]] 2,560x (40 stacks (2,560 items total)) [[Raw Cod]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Sponge]] 2,560x [[Raw Salmon]] 1,280x (20 stacks (1,280 items total)) [[Prismarine Shard]] 1,280x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 2,560x [[Clay Ball]] 2,560x [[Pufferfish]] 2,560x [[Tropical Fish]] 32x [[Frogcoin]] 8x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] 102,400x (1,600 stacks (102,400 items total)) [[Lotus]] 8x [[Unlucky Doubloon]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BUCKET_OF_FROGSPAWN
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Frog Pets from Legendary to Mythic.; Who cares about massive frogs when you can have lots of tiny ones?; MYTHIC
The Frogspawn Bucket is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item used to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Frog Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lily Pad XI (11) ([[Lily Pad]])|1x [[Lotus Water Orb]] 7x [[Condensed Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Condensed Lily Pad]] ×7 + [[Lotus Water Orb]] → Frogspawn Bucket|
## Usage
A Frogspawn Bucket can be used at [[Kat]] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Frog Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. This costs 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and takes 1h.
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Frog Pet]] unlocks the following [[Stats|Stat]] and perk:
|Perk|Increments|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trophy Chance]]|0.05% [[Trophy Chance]]|0.05% [[Trophy Chance]]|5% [[Trophy Chance]]|
|Home Sweet Home; Increases your chance of catching GOLD and DIAMOND Trophy Frogs by +x%|+0.1%|+0.1%|+10%|
## History

|May 14, 2026|Added Frogspawn Bucket.|

A Frozen Scute is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item obtained from looting a Tungsten, Umber, or Vanguard [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]].
## Obtaining
### Frozen Corpses
A Frozen Scute can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Frozen Scute]] 1 Frozen Scute|0.06% (1 in 1635)|
|Vanguard|[[Frozen Scute]] 1 Frozen Scute|0.28% (1 in 351.5)|
## Usage
### [[Armadillo Pet]]
A Frozen Scute can be used at [[Kat]] to upgrade [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armadillo Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. This costs 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and takes 1h.

## Obtaining
The Garden Warrior Fermento Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Hypixel Store as part of the 2026 Summer SkyBlock Bundle.

The Garden Warrior Fermento Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Fermento Armor|Fermento Helmet]] or [[Helianthus Armor|Helianthus Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. The Geode Mithril Golem Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Geode Mithril Golem Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 18, 2026. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Geode Mithril Golem Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Mithril Golem Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. - This skin will allow you to swap between 21 geode themed golems!

# Guardian Lucky Block
Categories: Mythic items, Items, Pets
[Guardian Lucky Block]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 30,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GUARDIAN_LUCKY_BLOCK
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Guardian Pets to Mythic rarity.; X Rift-Exportable X Loses motes value when crossing dimensions.; MYTHIC
The Guardian Lucky Block is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exportable #]] item used to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Guardian Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
## Obtaining
The Guardian Lucky Block may drop from killing the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] if the player is at least [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer IV (4)]].
|Boss Tier|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier III|6.25%|
|Tier IV|6.1%|
|Tier V|5.99%|
## Usage
The Guardian Lucky Block can be given to [[Kat]] alongside with 1,000,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Guardian Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Guardian Pet]] unlocks the following perk:
|Perk|Description|
|-|-|
|Lucky Seven|Gain 0.07%-7% chance to find ultra rare books in Superpairs.|
It also upgrades the Laserbeam ability:
|Rarity|Perk|Description|
|-|-|-|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Laserbeam|Zaps your enemies for 0.2x-20x your [[Intelligence]] every 3s.|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Laserbeam|Zaps your enemies for 1.2x-120x your [[Intelligence]] every 3s.|
## Trivia
- It has same texture as the Wild Lucky Block from Lucky Block SkyWars gamemode on Hypixel, despite Guardian Lucky Block in that game being blue.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Guardian Lucky Block.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Increased the rarity of Guardian Lucky Block from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Guardian Lucky Block.|
|Jun 26, 2023|Guardian Lucky Block is now # Rift-Exportable # ([[Rift Dimension]]) instead of # Rift-Transferable # ([[Rift Dimension]]).|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Added Guardian Lucky Block to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Increased the rarity of Guardian Lucky Block from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|EnGuardian Lucky Block can no longer be recombobulated.|

Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to Radiant Power Orb, Mana Flux Power Orb, Overflux Power Orb, and Plasmaflux Power Orb.; This skin is stacked two tall, and swappable between 16 variants!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Gumball Machine Power Orb Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Deployable Skins|Power Orb Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Gumball Machine Power Orb Skin can be purchased from the Winter 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Gumball Machine Power Orb Skin can be applied to a [[Radiant Power Orb]], [[Mana Flux Power Orb]], [[Overflux Power Orb]] or [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 16 different animated versions.
- This skin is stacked two tall, and swappable between 16 variants!

# Jerry 3D Glasses
Categories: Mythic items, Items
[Jerry 3D Glasses]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_TOY_JERRY
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Jerry Pets to Mythic rarity.; MYTHIC
The Jerry 3D Glasses are a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item obtained from [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]] that can upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Jerry Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rarity]].
## Obtaining
### Jerry Boxes
The Jerry 3D Glasses has a chance to be obtained from [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]].
|Type|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|Green ([[Jerry Box]])|1|0.44%|
|Blue ([[Jerry Box]])|1|0.44%|
|Purple ([[Jerry Box]])|1|0.43%|
|Golden ([[Jerry Box]])|1|0.81%|
## Usage
The Jerry 3D Glasses can be given to [[Kat]] along with 25,000 [[Coins]] to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Jerry Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. The coins required depends on the level of the pet when giving it to Kat.
Upgrading the [[Jerry Pet]] with the Jerry 3D Glasses will unlock the following:
|Perk|Increments|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|-|
|Jerry; Actually adds 𝑥 [[Damage]] to the [[Aspect of the Jerry]].|0.5 [[Damage]]|0.5 [[Damage]]|50 [[Damage]]|
|Jerry; Tiny chance to find Jerry Candies ([[Jerry Candy]]) when killing mobs.||||
## Trivia
- Prior to version 0.18.3, the [[Jerry Pet]] had to hold the Jerry 3D Glasses as a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] to be [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
  - Any [[Jerry Pet|Jerry Pets]] still holding the item when the update released were instantly upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and had its Jerry 3D Glasses removed as a held [[Pet Items|Pet Item]], effectively allowing a different [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] to be given to the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Jerry Pet]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Increased the rarity of Jerry 3D Glasses from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 11, 2021|Added Jerry 3D Glasses.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Jerry 3D Glasses are no longer categorized as a Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Jerry 3D Glasses to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Increased the rarity of Jerry 3D Glasses from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Jerry 3D Glasses can no longer be recombobulated.|
Linked from: [[Bazaar]], [[Bazaar/Item List]], [[Candidate Jerry]], [[Changelog/2021/January 11]], [[Jerry (disambiguation)]], [[Jerry Box]], [[Jerry Pet]], [[Kat]], [[Kat/Costs]], [[Pet Items]], [[Pet Upgrades]], [[Pets]]

The Monster Sheep Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Monster Sheep Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on September 13, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Monster Sheep Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Sheep Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 15 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 15 monster sheep!

The Oasis Dye can be purchased from the Spring 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]].

The Oasis Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #BAFF81 and ⁠ #B7FF84. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

The Oculus Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 6, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 4,000 purchases.

The Oculus Skin can be applied to a [[Precursor Eye]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 19 different animated versions.

# Plasma Bucket
Categories: Mythic items, Minion fuels, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Minion Modifiers
[Plasma Bucket]
Type: [[Minion Fuel]]
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Magma Bucket]]
Next →: [[Everburning Flame]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 204,800x (3,200 stacks (204,800 items total)) [[Coal]] 1,920x (30 stacks (1,920 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 3x [[Heat Core]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: Heat Cores ([[Heat Core]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PLASMA_BUCKET
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 35%. Unlimited Duration!; MYTHIC
The Plasma Bucket is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Minion Fuel]] that increases [[Minions|Minion]] speed by 35%.
It has an unlimited duration and can be taken from the minion at any time, without being destroyed. It is currently the second most efficient infinite fuel source, after the [[Everburning Flame]].
## Recipe
### Obtaining
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Heat Core]] 2x [[Magma Bucket]]|Recipe: [[Magma Bucket]] ×2 + [[Heat Core]] → Plasma Bucket|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Plasma Buckets increase minion speed by 35% and have an unlimited duration. Unlike consumable fuels they can be taken from [[Minions]] to be reused. Plasma Bucket can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Everburning Flame]]|Red Sand VIII (8) ([[Red Sand]])|[[Plasma Bucket]] 1x Plasma Bucket 2x [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] 2x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 16x [[Flames]]|Recipe: [[Flames]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] ×2 + Plasma Bucket → [[Everburning Flame]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Plasma Bucket as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

A Pure Mithril Gem is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item used to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mithril Golem Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv109]|[[Diamond Goblin]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
A Pure Mithril Gem can be given to [[Kat]] alongside 100,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mithril Golem Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Mithril Golem Pet]] unlocks the following perk:
|Perk|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|
|Refined Senses|Increases your [[Magic Find]] by 0.1% when on a Mining Island.|Increases your [[Magic Find]] by 10% when on a Mining Island.|
### [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contacts]]
A Pure Mithril Gem can be given to [[Gemma]] to become an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
### [[Salvaging]]
A Pure Mithril Gem can also be salvaged for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Diamond Essence]].

# Retro-Encabulating Visor
Categories: Mythic items, Armor, Pages with UIs
[Retro-Encabulating Visor]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Intelligence]]: -50
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RETRO_ENCABULATING_VISOR
Tooltip: Intelligence: -50; Lets you see presumably invisible cables when worn.; Kloon built this in a cave!; With a bunch of scraps!; MYTHIC HELMET
The Retro-Encabulating Visor is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
A Retro-Encabulating Visor can be claimed from [[Unhinged Kloon]] at the beginning of their quest. It can be re-purchased from them for 1,000 [[Motes]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Retro-Encabulating Visor [[Intelligence]]: -50
While wearing a Retro-Encabulating Visor, pathways from [[Unhinged Kloon]] to eight different terminals can be seen. Opening a terminal requires the player to hack it.
The hacking menu contains five numbers at the top (2-16), and five rows of moving numbered items. Each row corresponds to the top number that matches its offset, meaning the top row corresponds to the left-most number, and the bottom row corresponds to the right-most number.
Each row must be selected when its corresponding number matches the number vertically aligned with it. For instance, in the example UI shown below, the first row was selected and hacked because the top 12 matched the row 12 vertically.
Each row must be hacked within 75 seconds. Clicking a row when the numbers don't align will remove 10 seconds of time. Once all rows have been hacked, you must set that terminal to a specific color to make a rainbow when brought back to [[Unhinged Kloon]].
|Location|Coordinates|Color Required|
|-|-|-|
|Next to [[Grandma]]|-68.5, 75, -62.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Red|
|Next to the Alchemist ([[Alchemist (Rift)]])|-43.5, 71, -61.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Orange|
|Next to Kat ([[Kat (Rift)]])|-38.5, 71, -94.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Yellow|
|Next to Plumber Joe ([[Plumber Joe (Rift)]])|-61.5, 72, -82.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Green|
|In a [[Wilted Berberis]] patch|-32.5, 70, -134 ([[Coordinates]])|Aqua|
|Next to [[Skylark]] and [[Yoshua]]|-66, 70.2, -118.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Blue|
|Close to the [[Mirrorverse]] entrance|-88.5, 73, -114.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Purple|
|Next to [[Chef]] and [[Joey McPizza]]|-109.5, 73, -106.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Pink|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Hacking a terminal|You hacked the terminal! Color picking will open in 3 seconds...|
|Hacking a terminal|You hacked the presumably make-believe terminal! Color picking will open in 3 seconds...|
|Failing to hack a terminal|You failed to hack the terminal! Learn to hack from the very basics: CLICK|
|Setting a terminal to red|You've set the color of this terminal to RED!|
|Setting a terminal to orange|You've set the color of this terminal to ORANGE!|
|Setting a terminal to yellow|You've set the color of this terminal to YELLOW!|
|Setting a terminal to green|You've set the color of this terminal to GREEN!|
|Setting a terminal to aqua|You've set the color of this terminal to AQUA!|
|Setting a terminal to blue|You've set the color of this terminal to BLUE!|
|Setting a terminal to purple|You've set the color of this terminal to PURPLE!|
|Setting a terminal to pink|You've set the color of this terminal to PINK!|
## Trivia
- The description "Kloon built this in a cave! With a bunch of scraps!" is a reference to the movie Iron Man, in which a character states that "Tony Stark was able to build this in a cave! With a box of scraps!" The latter quote became an internet meme.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Retro-Encabulating Visor.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Retro-Encabulating Visor.|

Item ID: SCORCHED_BOOKS
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Scorching power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +271.42&#xE00D; Strength +310.2&#xE007; Crit Damage +58.16&#xE001; Attack Speed; Unique Power Bonus:; +7&#xE00B; Ferocity; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 35.; MYTHIC POWER STONE
Scorched Books is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Scorching]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Attack Speed]]. It also provides +7 [[Ferocity]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
The Scorched Books is a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from the Tier II [[Inferno Demonlord]] or above if the player has [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL II (2)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 2.26% (1/44.25)
- Tier III: 2.16% (1/46.25)
- Tier IV: 2.03% (1/49.21)
## Requirements
The Scorched Books requires [[Combat|Combat XXXV (35)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Scorched Books at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Scorching [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

## Tips
- Due to granting a lot of [[Attack Speed]] as well as [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]], it is recommended to use this power for anything melee, at least before unlocking [[Golden Dragon Pet]].
  - It is also the best [[Accessory Power]] for [[Ferocity]] making it useful against [[Enderman Slayer]], as well as [[Bosses]] which have a Damage cap.
- The Accessory Power granted by the [[Furball]] can also be used to grant [[Attack Speed]], since it's cheaper and [[Combat]] requirement is much lower, although the stats granted by it are worse.
- This item can be used even without [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer II (2)]].

Item ID: PET_SKIN_TURTLE_SHELL_SHULKED
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Turtle pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 16 favorite shulky shells!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Shell Shulked Turtle Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Shell Shulked Turtle Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 23, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Shell Shulked Turtle Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Turtle Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 16 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 16 favorite shulky shells!

The Sorrowful Samurai Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Sorrowful Samurai Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 1, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 4,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Sorrowful Samurai Skin can be applied to a [[Sorrow Armor|Sorrow Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 18 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 18 tearful versions!

# Vampire Fang
Categories: Mythic items, Items, Mob Drops
[Vampire Fang]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Uses: Upgrading LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]] to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_VAMPIRE_FANG
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Bat Pets to Mythic rarity.; MYTHIC
Vampire Fang is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item that can upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bat Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rarity]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv312]|[[Grim Reaper]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
## Usage
The Vampire Fang can be given to [[Kat]] along with 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bat Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. The coins required depends on the level of the pet when giving it to Kat.
Upgrading the [[Bat Pet]] with a Vampire Fang will unlock the following:
|Perk|Increments|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sea Creature Chance]]|0.05% [[Sea Creature Chance]]|0% [[Sea Creature Chance]]|5% [[Sea Creature Chance]]|
|Sonar; Grants a +x% to fish up spooky sea creatures.|+0.2%|+0.2%|+20%|
## Trivia
- Prior to version 0.18.3, the Vampire Fang was a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. [[Bat Pet|Bat Pets]] would hold a Vampire Fang to upgrade to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
  - Any Bat Pets still holding the item when the update released were instantly upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and had its Vampire Fang removed.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Increased the rarity of Vampire Fang from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 2, 2020|0.9.10|Added Vampire Fang.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Vampire Fang is no longer categorized as a Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Vampire Fang to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Increased the rarity of Vampire Fang from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Vampire Fang can no longer be recombobulated.|

The Wolf Pack Werewolf Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Wolf Pack Werewolf Skin can be purchased from the Fall 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Wolf Pack Werewolf Skin can be applied to a [[Werewolf Armor|Werewolf Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 18 different animated versions.
- This skin is swappable between 18 favorite wolf packs!

The Armadillo Pet is a [[Mining]] [[Pets|Mount]] [[Pets|Pet]] that is obtained via hatching [[Prehistoric Egg|Prehistoric Eggs]]. It can be ridden to break blocks in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
The Armadillo Pet hatches from a [[Prehistoric Egg]] after walking a certain amount of steps to hatch it. To obtain the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Armadillo Pet, one can use a [[Frozen Scute]] and 700,000 [[Coins]] to upgrade it at [[Kat]].
|Steps|Rarity|Chance|Overall chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|4,000|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|50%|50%|
|10,000|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50%|25%|
|20,000|RARE ([[Rarity]])|50%|12.5%|
|40,000|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|50%|6.25%|
|100,000|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|100%|6.25%|

## Usage
The Armadillo Pet takes the form of a head on the ground when summoned. It will attempt to follow the player around, but it moves extremely slowly. If the pet is too far away, it may teleport to its owner.
Right-clicking the pet allows the player to mount it, allowing them to control its movements. The pet has an energy meter which appears above the XP bar when riding it; this meter's capacity is decided on by the pet's rarity.
- [[Rarity|COMMON]]: 100
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]: 150
- [[Rarity|RARE]]: 200
- [[Rarity|EPIC]]: 250
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]: 300
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]: 400
The pet's energy depletes when the Armadillo breaks a block.
Energy Depletion:
|Block|Depletion|
|-|-|
|[[Mithril]]|1|
|Ruby ([[Gemstone]])|1.2|
|Sapphire ([[Gemstone]])|1.5|
|Jade ([[Gemstone]])|1.5|
|Amber ([[Gemstone]])|1.5|
|Amethyst ([[Gemstone]])|1.5|
|Opal ([[Gemstone]])|1.5|
|Topaz ([[Gemstone]])|1.8|
|Jasper ([[Gemstone]])|2|
|Everything Else|0.2|
When the pet's energy reaches 0, the Armadillo will stop moving, and stamina regen will halt. The stamina of the Armadillo naturally regenerates 0.33% of its maximum energy per second. The pet stops regenerating energy only while moving. It regenerates fully while riding so long as the player is stationary, as well as when they are not riding the pet.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Hatching|CONGRATULATIONS! Your prehistoric egg hatched into a <rarity> Armadillo!|
|Exhausted|Your armadillo is Exhausted! *pant pant*|
|Fully charged|Your armadillo is full of energy!|
|Rolling Miner|Rolling miner granted you double drops!|
|Disabled|The Armadillo Pet is currently disabled!|
## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]||3 Hours|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|30k [[Coins]]||10 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]||5 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1.5M [[Coins]]|1x [[Frozen Scute]]|1 Hour|
## Trivia
- The [[Armadillo Pet]] Armadillo Pet was the first to have five perks at [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity. It now shares this property with the [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Wisp Pet|Subzero Wisp Pet]] and [[Jade Dragon Pet]].
- While holding a [[Pickonimbus 2000]] or Drill, breaking blocks with the Armadillo Pet will use up durability/fuel. If the Pickonimbus breaks or the drill runs out of fuel, blocks will still be broken, but the tool's stats will not apply.
- The Armadillo used to not give any gemstones when using it to mine gemstones without a proper pickaxe, but breaking gemstones could still display "Rolling Miner granted you double drops" in chat.
- This pet can ride minecarts.
- It used to be possible to obtain a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Armadillo Pet via a glitch. This did not provide any additional perks, just pet score and SkyBlock XP.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Hunk of Ice]]|1 day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Hunk of Ice]]|2 days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Hunk of Ice]]|3 days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|8x [[Hunk of Blue Ice]]|4 days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Pocket-sized Igloo]]|1 hour|

# Bat Pet
Aliases: Mystery Bat Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Mushroom Collection, Mining pets
[Bat Pet]
Type: [[Mining]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mushroom III (3) ([[Mushroom]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: BAT
Tooltip: Mining Pet; Intelligence: +1➡100; Speed: +0.05➡5; Candy Lover Increases drop chance of candies from mobs by 0.1➡10%.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
A Bat Pet is a craftable [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|256x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 256x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|4x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 4x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 8x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 16x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|30 Minutes|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 32x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] 1x [[Vampire Fang]]|1 Hour|

## Trivia
- The Bat Pet was the first pet that could be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
  - Prior to version 0.18.3, the Bat Pet had to hold the [[Vampire Fang]], similar to [[Pet Items]], to be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
  - Any Bat Pets still holding the item when the update released were instantly upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and had their Vampire Fang removed.
- The texture of this pet is similar to the [[Bat Artifact]].
- Since most [[Spooky]] mobs are also [[Shielded]] and the damage dealt is rounded to a whole number of hits, the Wings of Steel ability may not actually increase damage dealt in practice without the use of a [[Blast o' Lantern]], [[Ghoul Buster]], or [[Pumpkin Launcher]].

[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.

## Trivia
- The Bee Pet design was created by bwnton.
- The Busy Buzz Buzz perk used to grant players a chance to obtain two [[Flowers]] for each one they broke while having the Bee Pet equipped. This has now been changed to instead increase Fortune stats.
- The Hive perk used to give only give buffs when other nearby players were also using the Bee pet. This has been changed to give buffs when any player is nearby, regardless of what pet they're using.
- The [[Bee Mask]] doubles the stats of this pet, including the granted [[Foraging Fortune]]. When paired with a [[Hephaestus Relic]], a Bee Pet becomes the third best pet for [[Foraging Fortune]], outclassed by the [[Frog Pet]] and [[Jade Dragon Pet]].
  - This can alternatively be paired with a [[Poignant Lucky Clover]] to become the second best source of [[Overbloom]] for crops, only outclassed by the [[Rose Dragon Pet]].

# Bingo Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Events, Bingo
[Bingo Pet]
Type: Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: BINGO
Tooltip: All Skills; Health: +1➡100; Strength: +0.25➡25; Speed: +0.75➡75; Lucky Looting Gain 5.2➡25% more collection items from any source!; Climber Gain 101.5➡250% more HOTM and HOTF XP.; This pet's perks are active even when the pet is not summoned!; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Bingo Pet is an All Skills [[Pets|Pet]] which is given upon creating a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile. The rarity of this pet is determined by the player's Ⓑ Bingo Rank. The pet is meant to help with the [[Bingo]] event.
## Obtaining
The Bingo Pet is automatically granted on [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles. The [[Rarity]] of this pet is determined by the player's [[Bingo|Bingo Rank]]. It cannot be upgraded by [[Kat]], but a [[Tier Boost]] can be used if the player obtains one.
All rarities of this pet require the same amount of XP as a [[Rarity|COMMON]] pet to level up.
|Bingo Rank|Pet Rarity|
|-|-|
|Ⓑ No Bingo Rank|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1)|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2)|RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3)|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
|Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4)|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4) and [[Tier Boost]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|

## Trivia
- This pet cannot be converted into an item.
- It was the first pet that boosted collection gains, the second one being the [[Black Cat Pet]] with [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
- It was the first pet that benefits from all skills, the second one being the [[Precursor Drone Pet]].
- It is currently the only pet in the game that is obtained directly by creating a new profile.
- Prior to a hotfix on April 8, 2022, it was possible to get the pet on a non-Bingo profile due to a bug. However, the pet's abilities do not work on non-Bingo profiles, and once it is added to the pet menu, it cannot be taken out. The item is tradeable and auctionable.
- Due to people obtaining the Bingo pet on normal profiles, it is now blacklisted outside of Bingo.
- All rarities of the pet level as if they were common rarity.
- It is one of the only pets whose abilities do not get increased with rarity.
- The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity of this pet is currently only obtainable using a [[Tier Boost]].
  - The first person to obtain a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Bingo pet was Jeeeegus.
  - He achieved this during the fourteenth Bingo on January 8th, 2023, with the help of many other Bingo players.

|May 1, 2022|All rarities of Bingo Pet require the same amount of XP to level up.|

# Black Cat Pet
Aliases: Black Cat, Cat Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Spooky Festival, Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Events, Combat pets
Spooky Festival Exclusive Black Cat Pet is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Black Cat Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Fear Mongerer]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2,000x (31 stacks plus 16 (2,000 in total)) [[Purple Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: BLACK_CAT
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Intelligence: +1➡100; Speed: +1.25➡125; Magic Find: +0.15➡15; Pet Luck: +0.15➡15; Hunter Increases your speed and speed cap by +1➡100.; Omen Grants 0.15➡15  Pet Luck.; Supernatural Grants 0.15➡15  Magic Find.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Black Cat Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] that is sold by the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 2,000 (31 stacks plus 16) [[Purple Candy]] during the [[Spooky Festival]]. It is mainly used to increase the [[Speed]] cap, [[Magic Find]], and [[Pet Luck]] and can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity using the [[Black Woolen Yarn]].
## Usage
### Shop Purchase
The Black Cat Pet can be purchased from the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 2,000 [[Purple Candy]] during the [[Spooky Festival]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Black Woolen Yarn]]|1 Hour|

## Bugs
- The Looting perk does not work on collection items that are dropped directly in compacted forms, such as [[Silver Magmafish]].
## Tips
- The Black Cat Pet is utilized in [[Dungeons]] by players seeking efficiency, as the pet gives a sizable increase in [[Speed]] and increases the [[Speed]] cap, which is highly useful in [[Dungeons]] as only one potion, including effects that increase the [[Speed]] stat, can be applied during each run, which significantly increases the difficulty of getting higher [[Speed]] inside [[Dungeons]].
- Due to increasing your speed cap by 100 with any [[Armor/Sets|armor set]], this pet can be paired with the [[Warden Helmet]] to boost its damage by +40% on top of its original bonus.
- The Black Cat Pet is often paired with Sorrow Armor for higher Magic Find, increasing the odds of rare drops.
## Trivia
- Upon release, the Black Cat Pet cost 2,500 (39 stacks plus 4) [[Purple Candy]], which made it impossible to purchase since a player's inventory is unable to hold that many candies. Upon further release, the Black Cat Pet price was reduced to 2,000 [[Purple Candy]], making it purchasable.
- For a couple months after the [[Spooky Festival]]'s release, [[George]] bought the Black Cat Pet for 20,000,000 [[Coins]], which was far more than its material cost, however this stayed in the game for multiple months because of the limited awareness of this exploit.
  - [[George]] now buys Black Cat Pets for 5,000,000 [[Coins]], which is roughly less than a fourth of its material cost.
- It looks almost identical to the [[Cat Talisman]] with its eyes being more blue than the teal eyes of the [[Cat Talisman]].
- In game, the Black Cat Pet improves your luck, however, in some places in real life, black cats are associated with bad luck and misfortune.
## History

|May 10, 2020|Black Cat Pet now costs 2000 [[Purple Candy]] from [[Fear Mongerer]] instead of 2500.|
|May 21, 2020|Increased Omen perk's [[Pet Luck]] to 15.|
|May 21, 2020|Clarified descriptions.|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Hunter, Omen and Supernatural stats are now shown as base stats to the pet.|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Black Cat Pet can now be upgraded to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) with [[Black Woolen Yarn]].|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Fixed the MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Black Cat Pet's Looting ability working on all drops, not just collection items.|

The Elephant is a [[Farming]] [[Pets|Pet]] that can be bought from [[Oringo]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] Event. It grants a bonus to [[Health]] and [[Intelligence]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Elephant Pet can be purchased from [[Oringo|Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper]] during the [[Traveling Zoo]] event. Its price is dependent on its [[Rarity]].
|Rarity|Price|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Coins]], 64 (1 stack) [[Acacia Log]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]], 1 [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]], 16 [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]], 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|15,000,000 [[Coins]], 512 (8 stacks) [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|15k [[Coins]]||6 hours|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|75k [[Coins]]||20 hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|900k [[Coins]]||5 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|14M [[Coins]]||10 Days|

## Trivia
- When it was originally released in v0.7.8, the Elephant Pet's Trunk Efficiency perk multiplied crop drops. In v0.9.11, its multiplier was increased from 1.2x to 1.6x, but it remained multiplicative due to a bug until it was converted to [[Farming Fortune]] in v0.11.0.
